USE InstructionFinder;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID('dbo.InstructionPart', 'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.InstructionPart
    (
        InstructionsInstructionID INT NOT NULL,
        PartsPartID INT NOT NULL,

        CONSTRAINT PK_InstructionPart 
            PRIMARY KEY (InstructionsInstructionID, PartsPartID),

        CONSTRAINT FK_InstructionPart_Instructions
            FOREIGN KEY (InstructionsInstructionID)
            REFERENCES dbo.Instructions(InstructionID),

        CONSTRAINT FK_InstructionPart_Parts
            FOREIGN KEY (PartsPartID)
            REFERENCES dbo.Parts(PartID)
    );
END
